(Written by Father Quinn) The numbering of the prophecies has nothing to do
with their order, and I have tried to group them together by the ones that
are similar in nature. Again, the numbering has nothing to do with order at
all, it is simply a means to know who is talking about which one.

1) Though planted, the seed shall not grow without the nectar blessed by 
Coryssa. 


2) Only nectar carried from the land newly under the wing of the empire
will allow Mother's wish to be fulfilled.


3) For the silver beechmast shall be rooted when the figurehead of the 
church's new order is in place. This will signify the dawn of the rebirth 
of Mother's word.


4) Mothers light will shine on the place her worship would be done. This 
serves as a warning, not a blessing, that the seed shall not sprout. 
Only from another of Mother's places shall the seed be nurtured.

5) One of the lost ones shall be the means by which the nectar returns home. 
His words and actions alone will give rise to the fall of Mother's people. 
It is then that the journey of the light and dark sisters shall begin.

  
6a) In the dawn of the new church, in the eyes of its people, will appear a 
triumvirate of lost ones.  Though bonded together as family, they are not 
alike. They will grow as normal children unless the brother becomes a vessel 
for Mother. Should this happen the seeds of their birthrights will begin to 
sprout through no fault of the brother. Only one shall Mother be able to save
and take under her wing to guide, the other shall be stolen away by evil and 
led on a descent into darkness.


6b) Once the blood of the brother coats the hands of the dark sister, the lost 
ones will become truly lost forever, and the blood shall forever distinguish 
light from dark in Mother's eyes. Mother will continue to guide her daughter 
of the light, the other shall become Mothers greatest foe, destroying the 
church and all its flock.

7) The aelf shall feel the pain of her spirit partner when it is taken from 
its brothers and sisters. Unbeknownst to the dark sister,  the key to the pain 
of the aelf is hidden away in a prison of the dark ones making. The evil that 
courses through her shall rejoice at the chance to overcome the hurdle that 
could be her demise. Though completely innocent, the one of light is vital to 
the pain of the aelf. 


8) For in the time of the snake, there will appear visions to the flock of 
believers. Though from the Devourer, they will believe, and the seed will be 
planted that destroys the soul of the one most worthy.


9) During a time of change, visions will appear to those who wish to believe. 
Hallucinations they will be, and who so ever believes in them will cause the 
death of the great forest.

the church.

[Under Keep, basement]
Flickering shadows dance across an impressive marble arch to the south. Light
flows through an opening to the north that leads back into Malthesia's
Laboratory. Cobwebs obscure most of the arch, while dust lies thick and heavy
upon the floor of the stone chamber. Faint noises emanate from within the dark
interior of the mysterious arch...

J> look wolfb
Before you stands Wolfbane Crag, Guildmaster of the Crimson Band and first
Lord of Fayd. His clearly wolfling features are highlighted by warm hazel eyes
that hint at a gentleness hidden by his rugged exterior.
He is wearing a finely crafted crimson tabard embroidered with the gleaming
silver eagle of the Crimson Band, some silver edged elkyri bracers engraved
with a battle scene from the war with Siryx, some blackened banded scarlet
boots, some crimson steel bracers inset with a pair of small emeralds in each
one, some blackened deeplander armor emblazoned with the flying eagle of the
Crimson Band upon the chest, a bright blue pin that says "I WAS EATEN!" on it,
a ruby studded silver ring of the Crimson Band, some heavy crimson thigh guards
polished to a high lustre, a heavy braided gold torc capped with spheres of
polished amber, a blackened elkyri helm emblazoned with a screaming silver
eagle, some blackened leather gloves each with a silver dragon embroidered on
the top, a heavy gold wedding ring of a majestic crimson eagle gently grasping
a blushing silver rose in its talon, a black and red cape with a large eagle
stitched into the back and a small gold pin shaped like a feather.
He is wielding a finely crafted albescent longsword with fiery randar forming a
pommel shaped into a screaming eagle's head and a fiery randar papenheimer.
He is behind a wall of force.
He radiates with magical strength.
He is surrounded by a prismatic globe.
He is outlined in glowing armor.
He is protected by a ghostly, hovering shield.
He is the center of a large, white sphere of light.
He appears to be healthy.
He has a singed head and a singed right hand.

[Under Keep]
A fiery reddish-orange light emanates from the walls to illuminate this section
of the passage. The polished black floor eerily reflects the glow, giving it
the appearance of having tiny fire elementals dancing along its surface. The
light flickers up towards the ceiling like a fire that is burning out of
control, exposing the rows upon rows of leering skulls that cover the entire
roof. A palpable inky darkness lies to the east while the passage opens up into
a larger room to the west.

large bloodstone, an ice sword, an ice sword, a coffer, an ice sword, a chest,
an ice sword, an ice sword, a marble flask, an ice sword, an ice sword, a glass
vial, an ice sword and a moonstone are here.

[Deep Forest]
The area was once marshy, and has now finally dried out completely. 
The trees seem to buckle up, and the ground is the consistency of sand.

the ground is buried under winter's chill. A trail leads south through an

> look rion
You see Rion SunStar, First Tracker, a handsome teenage wolfling with sandy
blond hair, and sapphire blue eyes that sparkle with the innocence of youth.
He moves with adolescent grace, and newly developed muscle.
He is wearing some gleaming white shoulder guards studded with blazing yellow
topaz, some gleaming white shin guards studded with blazing yellow topaz, a
blackened steel scabbard trimmed in tarnished silver, some supple leather
gloves with small squares of metal sewn into the back for protection, a
sterling silver pin shaped like a flight of dragons soaring through the center
of a star, a mysterious ring, some small black boots, a steel gray helm inset
with a gleaming silver wolf on one side, some gleaming dark deeplander armor
etched with a wolf silhouetted against a full moon and a heavy silver signet
ring of the Faydian Border Guard.
He is wielding a fiery albescent broadsword with a hilt which glows with a
shimmering sea-green light and a fiery randar longsword.
He is behind a wall of force.
He radiates with magical strength.
He appears to be healthy.
He has a lacerated and bludgeoned head, a lacerated and scorched body, a
lacerated and burnt back, a nicked and grazed right arm, a lacerated, grazed
and singed left arm, a nicked and banged-up right leg, a nicked, grazed and
scorched left leg, a nicked and banged-up right hand and a lacerated,
banged-up, grazed and scorched left hand.

[Under Keep]
A fiery reddish-orange light emanates from the walls to illuminate this section
of the passage. The polished black floor eerily reflects the glow, giving it
the appearance of having tiny fire elementals dancing along its surface. The
light flickers up towards the ceiling like a fire that is burning out of

a larger room to the west.

[Under Keep, hallway]
A thick coat of black ice covers the entirety of this hallway, making the
footing treacherous and uneven. Sharp, jagged icicles hang from the walls and
ceiling, whilst some can be seen protruding from the floor. The dried blood
present upon a few icy points gives testimony that this portion of the hallway
is not for the unwary. The light comes from a few of the icy stalagmites that
glow with a deep red hue. The hallway continues on its frozen trail to the
northeast and southwest.

[Under Keep, ice sculptures]
Cold tendrils of a wispy mist flow around numerous sculptures that are randomly
placed in this wide corridor. Swirling on the mist are black snowflakes, a few
of them gently descending only to disappear in a burst of intense flame as they
touch the icy floor of the passage. The sculptures have all been formed from
the same black ice that clings to the walls and ceiling of the corridor, and
they all seem strangely familiar.

You revel in the tumult as the center of the bazaar overwhelms your senses
with its smell and sizzle of roast mutton, its cries of hawkers and cajoling
merchants. Street dancers, minstrels, and half-clad jugglers amuse the
crowds near a large marble fountain.  A large statue is almost as formidable
a presence as the fountain.

and a newsboard are here.

[Bazaar Center]
You revel in the tumult as the center of the bazaar overwhelms your senses
with its smell and sizzle of roast mutton, its cries of hawkers and cajoling
merchants. Street dancers, minstrels, and half-clad jugglers amuse the
crowds near a large marble fountain.  A large statue is almost as formidable
a presence as the fountain.
You see a wolf and a commoner. A wooden bin and a newsboard are here.
Obvious exits: north, south, east, west, northeast, northwest, southeast,
southwest.
